# Plot an Ecological Network Organized by Trophic Level using visNetwork

This function visualizes a food web using `visNetwork`, where nodes
represent species and edges represent interactions. Nodes are positioned
by trophic level on the y-axis, and optionally, community modules on the
x-axis. Unlike
[`plot_troph_level_ggplot()`](https://lsaravia.github.io/multiweb/reference/plot_troph_level_ggplot.md)
and
[`plot_troph_level_ggraph()`](https://lsaravia.github.io/multiweb/reference/plot_troph_level_ggraph.md),
this produces an interactive HTML widget that supports dragging,
zooming, hovering for details, and node search.

## Usage

``` r
plot_troph_level_visNet(
  g,
  vertexSizeMin = 8,
  vertexSizeMax = 25,
  modules = FALSE,
  weights = NA,
  community_obj = NULL,
  node_weights = NA,
  module_spacing = 350,
  physics = c("x", "full"),
  highlight = TRUE,
  search = TRUE,
  label_size = 16,
  width = "100%",
  height = "850px"
)
```

## Arguments

- g:

  An `igraph` object representing the ecological network.

- vertexSizeMin:

  Minimum node size (default: 8).

- vertexSizeMax:

  Maximum node size (default: 25).

- modules:

  Logical; if `TRUE`, nodes are grouped by community modules along the
  x-axis (default: `FALSE`).

- weights:

  Edge weights used for community detection when `modules = TRUE` and
  `community_obj` is not supplied (default: `NA`).

- community_obj:

  Optional pre-computed community detection object (e.g. from
  `cluster_spinglass()`). If `NULL` and `modules = TRUE`, community
  detection is run internally via `cluster_spinglass()`.

- node_weights:

  Optional numeric vector used to size nodes. If `NA` (default), node
  degree is used.

- module_spacing:

  Horizontal spacing multiplier between community modules when
  `modules = TRUE` (default: 350). When `modules = FALSE`, node
  x-coordinates are instead spread across a wide random range scaled by
  `module_spacing * 1.5`.

- physics:

  Layout mode, one of `"x"` or `"full"` (default: `"x"`).

  - `"x"`: during the simulation, nodes can move horizontally but their
    vertical position stays fixed at their trophic level, so the layout
    settles into trophic bands.

  - `"full"`: nodes move freely in both x and y under a force-directed
    layout (`forceAtlas2Based`), anchored by a small `centralGravity` to
    prevent unbounded drift.

  In both modes, once stabilization finishes, physics is disabled and
  every node's `fixed.x`/`fixed.y` is unlocked, so the trophic-level
  ordering (in `"x"`) or the force-directed layout (in `"full"`) is just
  the starting point — afterwards any node can be freely dragged in any
  direction.

- highlight:

  Logical; if `TRUE`, hovering over a node highlights its nearest
  neighbors (default: `TRUE`).

- search:

  Logical; if `TRUE`, adds a node-search dropdown to the widget
  (default: `TRUE`).

- label_size:

  Font size, in pixels, used for node labels (default: 16). Apparent
  label size can differ between `physics` modes even at the same value,
  since `"full"` typically stabilizes into a more spread-out layout that
  is zoomed further out to fit the view; adjust this parameter if labels
  look too large or too small for a given mode.

- width:

  Width of the `visNetwork` widget, passed through to
  [`visNetwork::visNetwork()`](https://rdrr.io/pkg/visNetwork/man/visNetwork.html)
  (default: `"100%"`). Accepts any CSS width string (e.g. `"600px"`,
  `"100%"`).

- height:

  Height of the `visNetwork` widget, passed through to
  [`visNetwork::visNetwork()`](https://rdrr.io/pkg/visNetwork/man/visNetwork.html)
  (default: `"850px"`). Accepts any CSS height string. When embedding in
  slide frameworks like `xaringan`, set an explicit pixel height small
  enough to fit the slide (e.g. `"400px"`) — the widget does not shrink
  to fit its container automatically.

## Value

A `visNetwork` HTML widget visualizing the trophic structure of the
network.

## Examples

``` r
#
# Example using the Kefi 2015 multiplex dataset shipped with the
# 'multiweb' package (trophic + positive + negative interaction layers)
#
fileName <- system.file("extdata", package = "multiweb")
dn <- list.files(fileName, pattern = "^Kefi2015.*\\.txt$")
g <- readNetwork(dn, fileName, skipColumn = 2)
names(g) <- c("Negative", "Positive", "Trophic")

plot_troph_level_visNet_multi(g, physics = "full")
```
